  --to widened to 2026-11-17 (today) so reports published after the window that describe in-window periods are caught.

Full-text (title+body, AND) checks inside the ERI-tagged set, same window:
  cholera 7 | choléra 7 (same 7; search is accent-insensitive) | cólera 0 | الكوليرا 0 | diarrhoea 0 | diarrhea 0 | AWD 0 | watery 0 |
  Vibrio 0 | outbreak 12 | epidemic 1 | disease 13. The 7 'cholera' bodies = OCHA Eastern Africa snapshots (Dec 2025, Mar, Jun,
  Oct 2026), UNICEF ESAR HSR No.1 2026 and No.2 end-2025, UNHCR Yemen Protection Brief 2025 - all opened (below).
Composition of the 214 (by source): UNHCR 145 (refugee/asylum dashboards & fact sheets - Eritreans in Sudan, Ethiopia, Kenya,
  Uganda, Rwanda, Egypt, Libya, Tunisia, Italy sea arrivals); FAO Desert Locust Bulletins / Locust Watch 20; IGAD/WMO/ECHO/ENSO/
  FSNWG/WHH/CGIAR climate & environment 11; OCHA 9; UNICEF/UNFPA 7; IOM/DTM/MMC 7; FAO other 4 (GIEWS, lumpy skin disease,
  displacement); MSF 2; IFRC 1 (Djibouti); advocacy/other 8 (GCR2P x2, joint NGO HRC letter, USCRI, RVI, SOS Mediterranee, ACF, WFP).
  NOT ONE report in 2026 is from WHO (any office), the Eritrean Ministry of Health, a Health Cluster, or an IFRC Eritrea
  operation, and none is an Eritrea-specific health/outbreak product.
Attachment screen (beyond titles): 200 of the 214 reports carry PDFs (201 files). All 201 were obtained: 16 via
  `py/reliefweb.py get <id> --download cache/reliefweb/ERI`, 185 by their attachment URL from the search listing (no extra API
  calls; 34 of these needed a slow retry after HTTP 429). Text via pdftotext; image-only files noted. Grep for
  cholera|choléra|cólera|acute watery|AWD|diarrh|Vibrio|الكوليرا|ሸሮኽ|ኮሌራ -> hits in 8 attachments, NONE located in Eritrea

=== PER-REPORT SCREENING TABLE (every enumerated report: date | id | url_alias | title | decision) ===
Codes: OPENED = body + attachments read (reason given); A1 = ERI-tagged, attachment text-screened, no cholera/AWD/diarrhoea/
  Vibrio term; A2 = ERI-tagged, attachment has a disease term but not Eritrea-located (reason given); B1 = ERI-tagged body-only,
  full-text check negative; T1 = title screen, single-country report on another country, no Eritrea+disease co-mention in body;
  T2 = title screen, multi-country/regional item that is not a country-level African cholera product, no Eritrea+disease
  co-mention in body. Decisions: ROW / PRESENCE / NO ERI DATA / OUT OF WINDOW.
